  BYTE-FRACTION CONTROL (board #501) — a `match` TU is byte-identical to c2's obj, so the port produced a body for every `.text` byte in it and its fraction MUST read 100%. 3 of 3 matched TUs do; 0 fall short; 0 have no `.text` denominator (a TU that defines data and no functions — board #276's shape — which is a 100%-free zero, not a failure).
    no shortfall. (A count, not a status: the 3 is the evidence.)
  FRONTIER BY CFG REACHABILITY (board #720) — CAN THE EMITTER EXPRESS THIS TU AT ALL? `Selected` covers THREE control-flow shapes: straight-line, ONE two-arm conditional, and — since lane `w-hash`, board #761 — ONE loop, the pointer-walk accumulate of `codegen::ptr_walk_loop`. **That third one is a transcription of a single function class, not a loop lowering**: twenty words, two immediate fields, `/O1` only, and every other loop shape still has no representation at all. This line read `no variant encodes a backward branch, so NO loop of any kind has a representation` until `Sort.cpp` converted; the correction is here rather than beside the old claim. This is not a quantity of progress like #269/#465/#500 — a TU can be one 8-byte function from matching and be unreachable because those 8 bytes are a loop.     LABEL CHANNEL (board #742) — `$M`/`$T` are the ONLY way the value of c2's compiler-label counter reaches an obj, and `coff::plan_labels` mints them for a FRAMED function and nothing else. A leaf loop charges that counter +1..+4 while `plan_labels` charges 0 (17 seed-free cells, docs/LABEL_COUNTER.md §4.2), which is the whole stated justification for `codegen::labels` invariant 4 refusing every BACKWARD branch. On a `label-free` obj that error has nowhere to land — and that is now SHIPPED rather than observed: `IlFunction::label_slots` returns `None` for the one loop shape the port emits, so a TU pairing it with a framed function refuses and a TU without one converts (boards #746/#747, `fixtures/cpp/whash_loop_then_framed.cpp` and its control). 15 of 744 frontier TUs are label-free; of the 0 blocked on `cflow-loop`, 0 are. NOT a licence — every one is still gated on codegen that does not exist, and the counter is only the FIRST of that TU's refusals.
    CONTROL src/xdk/nuispeech/xboxmem.cpp: absent from this scan's list — NOT a pass, not evaluated.
    SUB-CLASS NARROWING (board #778) — the screen can now hold a PARTIAL claim (`CfgClass{class, Whole | Keys(&[..])}`), and this is the bracket that keeps a restriction NARROWER OR EQUAL rather than wider. `admits` is `class == class && <sub>`, so `Keys` only ever CONJOINS: a restriction can remove an admitted (class,key) pair and never add one. That is algebra; these four numbers are the measurement, re-derived from this scan's own frontier. BOTTOM 0 ⊆ ENUMERATED 726 == SHIPPED 726 ⊆ TOP 744, of 744 frontier TUs (3 classes in TOP, 5 (class,key) pairs enumerated).
      BOTTOM=every entry restricted to NO keys (must be 0 — it is the live exercise of the `Keys` path and the detector for a matcher that ignores its key); ENUMERATED=every entry rewritten as the exact key set this scan observed (must equal SHIPPED); TOP=every class the frontier mentions, wholesale — a HYPOTHETICAL the port has no claim to, printed so the refusal has a size: 18 of the 744 frontier TUs are held back by CFG class alone.

  PROGRESS MASS — a PROGRESS metric, NEVER a correctness signal (docs/PROGRESS_METRIC.md).
    The byte-exact differential is the SOLE judge; `match` below is the only correctness count.
    No term rewards emitted bytes: P counts proven preconditions (A, B, C against the reference obj)
    and honest acceptance (the emitted census). A TU graded `mismatch` contributes 0 to every
    numerator, so a wrong emit always scores below the refusal it replaced.
    P = mean(a, b, c, f) over 747 graded TUs = 0.75100
      a  emit-set reachable (A)         747/747  = 1.00000

  FUNCTION BYTE MATCH (FBM) — a PROGRESS instrument, NEVER a gate (docs/FUNCTION_BYTE_MATCH.md).
    The judge's own predicate — byte-identical to real c2 — asked per EMITTED FUNCTION instead of
    per TU. No partial credit: a wrong body scores exactly what a refusal scores, which is 0.
    The denominator is counted off c2's obj, so refusing more never shrinks it.
    FBM = (3 + 0)/747 emitted functions byte-exact = 0.00402
      exact              3  ( 0.40%)   CREDITED — per-function route, bytes identical to c2's
      whole-TU           0  ( 0.00%)   CREDITED — on a TU the differential graded `match`; the judge certified the whole obj
      differs            0  ( 0.00%)   complete port body, bytes differ
      reloc-diff         0  ( 0.00%)   bytes IDENTICAL, RELOCATIONS DIFFER — two bodies branching to two different functions (board #884)
      reloc-unk          0  ( 0.00%)   bytes identical, reference relocation table did not decode — UNGRADED, never credited
      partial            0  ( 0.00%)   selected; the PORT's own /Gy composition declined the body (board #322 closed the harness's half)
      refused          744  (99.60%)   the port declines the function
      unbound            0  ( 0.00%)   no census row claims the symbol
      no-bytes           0  ( 0.00%)   COMDAT raw data did not decode
    objs unreadable (contribute NO denominator): 0   partition breaks (known answer 0): 0
    KNOWN-ANSWER CONTROL — per-function bodies that DIFFER on a TU the oracle graded `match` (must be 0): 0
    FIVE-ALARM CONTROL — byte-exact bodies whose RELOCATIONS differ on a `match` TU (must be 0): 0
    census/gate disagreement on EMITTED fns (the error term on the emitted census, target 0): 0
    NOTE: the buckets partition the denominator by the PER-FUNCTION route alone; on a `match` TU the whole-obj verdict supersedes them.
